## Android Community Screens And Late Answers Checkpoint

For the prerequisite of [T82](TASKS.md#defects-that-break-approved-requirements), in the building session: before the app-wide request lock can be narrowed, a screen must not let an answer read before a change arrive after the change and undo it. This covers the Android community screen (feed, pages and posts, a page, a post, Your pages and Blocked) and the moderation screen.

- **The risk on the community screen.** Like, Save, Follow, Pin, Publish, comments and the other changes stay available while a list or page loads; only Refresh and Load more wait. Today the lock makes a change wait until the load has answered. Without it, the load's older answer could arrive after the change: a like or a pin undone on screen until the next load, and with Load more on a page or a post, the page or the post put back as it was when Load more was pressed.
- **Fix** (`CommunityViewModel.kt`):
  - A change stops a load that is still unanswered and sends it again once the change is answered, as Load more if it was Load more, unless the change reloads the screen itself.
  - If the change failed, the load sent again keeps the change's message.
  - Refresh is not sent while a change is unanswered. The buttons already waited; this covers every caller.
  - A stopped load no longer marks the screen as loaded while the load sent after it is still out.
- **The moderation screen already met it:** it sends no decision, resolution or appeal while anything on it loads, and loads nothing while one is unanswered. Two tests now show it; its code is unchanged.
- **Not changed:** the lock itself. Before it can be narrowed, these still need the same check: chat, reminders, events, tasks and documents, which the analysis named, and the other screens that refresh.

| Check (2026-10-02) | Result |
| --- | --- |
| New tests | `CommunityTest`, 4: a like while the feed loads stays liked; a refused like keeps its message and the feed still loads; a like while more comments load keeps the post liked and still adds the comments, asking for the same next page; Refresh during an unanswered like sends nothing. `ModerationTest`, 2: Refresh during an unanswered decision sends nothing; a decision is not sent while the queue loads. The simulated server reads the post when a list is asked for and can hold that answer, so the change is answered first. |
| After the fix | In a separate copy: `CommunityTest` **81 of 81** and `ModerationTest` **26 of 26**; then the 6 new tests 10 more times, **60 of 60**. The first run of the first three new tests had one failure: the Load more test saw the screen idle before the Load more sent again had answered, because the stopped load's last step cleared the loading mark (in tests the screen's code runs on several threads). That check now runs inside the state update. A review then found that the load sent again cleared a failed change's message; it now keeps it, and the refused-like test covers it. |
| The tests can fail | Four runs in another copy, the shared tree untouched (`.local/t82c/mutation-*.txt`). With the lock held only to read and clear the session, as T82 plans, and without this change: **exactly the 6 new tests failed** and the other 101 passed. The like was undone by the feed's older answer, the post lost its like when more comments arrived, Refresh and the moderation Refresh were sent, the refused like's load was not sent again, and a decision was sent while the queue loaded. With the narrowed lock and this change: **107 of 107**. With today's lock and without this change: the same 6 failed, three of them because the like waited for the load. With only the kept message undone: only the refused-like test failed ("expected Synthetic CONFLICT but was null"). |

**Boundary.** Not run on a device: the community and moderation screen tests use fixed screen states, and the screens' code did not change. Evidence: `.local/t82c/`.

## Android Chat, Care And Other Screens And Late Answers Checkpoint

Continues the [community checkpoint](#android-community-screens-and-late-answers-checkpoint) for [T82](TASKS.md#defects-that-break-approved-requirements), in the building session: the other Android screens that refresh, apart from events, checklists and the calendar, which T117 is changing.

- **Chat had two real risks.** A page of messages read before a deletion could bring the deleted message back, and the conversation list read before a chat was marked read could show it unread again. Fix: a deleted copy of a message is kept whichever copy arrives last (`MessagingModels.kt`), and a change to a chat stops a list read still unanswered and reads the list again quietly (`MessagingViewModel.kt`). A stopped list read no longer clears the loading marks of the read sent after it.
- **Care had the community risk** (a dose note undone by the day's older answer) and got the community fix in `CareViewModel.kt`.
- **Already safe, now shown by tests:** documents, tasks, reminders, account settings, checklists and the agent send no change while a read is out and no read while a change is out; "Your data" also stops a list read when a change starts. Search, Home and the calendar only read, one request at a time.
- **Still to check:** events (needs the community fix), after T117. Checklists and the calendar were done without waiting, because T117 was changing only their screens. At 20:09 T117 also changed the checklist view model, adding a message counter; its request guards are unchanged, and `ChecklistTest`, now with T117's test too, passes 10 of 10 on the tree with that change. **Events:** the community fix and 4 tests are ready in a private copy and wait for T117, which is changing `EventsViewModel.kt` and `EventsTest.kt`. There `EventsTest` passes 17 of 17 with today's lock and with the narrowed one; without the fix exactly the 4 new tests fail, and with the narrowed lock a response is undone by the list's older answer ("expected going but was null").
- **Found on the way, not changed:** two identical tracked `HomeViewModel.kt` files, in `feature/home` and `feature/platform`; the `feature/home` copy is imported nowhere.

## Android Moderator Test Repaired Checkpoint

Builds [T119](TASKS.md#defects-that-break-approved-requirements), found by the audit session. `CommunityScreenTest.largeTextNarrowModeratorPinsButHasNoOwnerControlsAndReadOnlyHidesThem`, added with page moderators in `642e47b`, had failed in every device run since. At 200% text on a screen 320 dp wide and 640 dp high, the page's header is taller than the screen. After the test showed the Follow button in the header, the list had let go of the post below it, so pressing the post's Pin button found no node.

- **What the failure hid:** just before that, the test checked that a moderator has no Edit or Delete on the post and none of the owner's sections, by counting nodes. The post was not on screen, so those checks would have passed with the controls shown. The check that a read-only page offers no Pin had the same gap.
- **Fix, in the test only:** a new `absent(tag)` looks for a tag through the whole list. Compose's `performScrollToNode` scrolls to the top of a lazy list and then one screen at a time to its end, and fails with "No node found that matches" when no item has the tag; `absent` expects exactly that failure. The moderator test uses it for every control that must be missing, shows the post on the read-only page before checking it has no Pin, and scrolls back to Pin before pressing it. The screen did not change.
- **Why it had no task:** the [T105 checkpoint](#android-messages-brought-into-view-checkpoint) recorded the failure as part 5 of T115, but that part never reached T115's row, so T115 was finished without it.
- **Committed separately from other work:** the completion session's uncommitted tests for read-only posts (T114) are in the same file; only this change's hunks were committed.

| Check (2026-10-02, a copy of commit `83e9068`, read-only API 36 emulator, network off) | Result |
| --- | --- |
| `CommunityScreenTest` with two tests kept in the copy only | **13 of 14** passed in 329 s (`.local/t119/device.txt`): the repaired test and the other 11. |
| The test as it was (copy only) | Failed as in every earlier run: `performScrollTo()` found no node with the post's Pin tag. |
| Can a count miss a control? (copy only) | Yes. After the Follow button was shown, a count of the post's Pin found none while the Pin was in the list; `absent` failed for that Pin and for Follow, as it should, and passed for the owner's Edit page button, which a moderator does not have. |

**Boundary.** Only this test changed. Whether other device tests count nodes after scrolling in the same way was not checked here; [T122](#android-absence-checks-look-through-the-whole-list-checkpoint) then checked them.

## Android Absence Checks Look Through The Whole List Checkpoint

Builds [T122](TASKS.md#defects-that-break-approved-requirements), found by the audit session. After [T119](#android-moderator-test-repaired-checkpoint), a read-only review on Claude Opus 5.5 followed each of the 230 checks that something is missing, in 14 Android device test classes, to the list item that would hold it (`.local/absence-review/report.md`). 19 could pass with the control shown, because a lazy list composes only the items near the screen and the test had scrolled elsewhere. 11 more depended on the screen's height.

- **Fix, in the tests only:** `assertNotInList(list, matcher)` in `ScreenChecks.kt` expects Compose's `performScrollToNode`, which scrolls to the top of a lazy list and then a screen at a time to its end, to find nothing. The Community, Space and Group screen tests call it through a short `absent`: for 18 of the 19 checks, for 3 of the height-dependent ones, and for 2 more checks in the same lines. The `absent` that T119 added now uses it too. The list is left at its end, so a test reveals what it presses next.
- **Not changed:**
  - Two height-dependent checks whose item has left the screen's state, so no screen rule can bring it back: a post result after switching to pages, and a member after removal.
  - The live `AccountJourneyTest` checks, which need the local services.
  - `CalendarScreenTest`, which the completion session is changing for T117.
- **How it was tested:** a copy of commit `2c96447` with the change, plus copy-only switches in the four screens. Each switch breaks one rule, for example showing the invite form while an invitation waits. Copy-only classes ran the current and the old version of each test with one switch on.

| Check (2026-10-02, read-only API 36 emulator, network off, 320 × 640 dp) | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Switches off | `CommunityScreenTest` **12 of 12**, `GroupScreenTest` **15 of 15**, `SpaceScreenTest` **22 of 23** (`.local/t120/device.txt`). The failure is `invitePolicyConfirmStaysReachableAtLargeText`, a Space settings test that compares a whole description with its first sentence. The design session has corrected it in the working copy but not committed it; this change does not touch it. |
| One switch on, the current tests | All 11 failed at the changed check, naming the control that was shown. |
| One switch on, the old tests | They missed the control in 5 of the 11 cases: the pinned heading after the last unpin; the moderator panel and page state on a deleted page; Edit page and the composer on a read-only page (caught only at the next check, the invite form); "You do not moderate any pages." beside roles and offers; and "You have not asked to join any group." beside requests. They caught it in the other 6: the empty followed-pages message while loading, before or after the loading line; a second moderator invitation while one waits; Remove on the owner, for a member; "Show more groups" after the last page; and the invite form after member invites are turned off. That last one stopped on the emulator's font scale change the first time, and failed at its check in two later runs (`.local/t120/device-rerun.txt`). |

**Boundary.** The switches exist only in the copy. Whether an old check missed depends on the screen size: some that the review expected to miss caught the control at 320 × 640 dp, and might not on another screen.

## Android Messages Announced And Shown Again Checkpoint

Builds parts 3 and 4 of [T117](TASKS.md#defects-that-break-approved-requirements), the smaller gaps the audit session found after T105 ([checkpoint](#android-messages-brought-into-view-checkpoint)). Done by the completion session with two background agents on GPT-6.1 Sol.

- **Part 3, live regions:** the error and notice on the Events and Checklist screens, and the error on the Calendar screen, are now polite live regions, as the other screens' messages already were.
- **Part 4, the same message again:** each screen scrolled to its message when the message's text changed. When an action ended with the same text as the one before, such as "Show more" failing twice, nothing changed, so the list did not move and the person saw nothing. Each screen's state now counts its messages (`messageId`): every new error or notice adds 1, and the list scrolls on every new message, even with the same text.
  - Done on Events and Space, which the audit named, and on Tasks, Checklists, Reminders and Group Spaces. On those four a check found that a refused entry repeats its text with nothing in between: a task title or notes that are too long, a checklist item title that is empty, too long or not plain text, a date and time that does not exist, or control characters in a join note.
  - Care, the calendar and moderation clear their message before each action, so the same text again is already a change. The check first reported moderation as affected, but its `command` clears the message before sending, so it was not changed.
  - The Community screen repeats in the same way, in its checks before creating a page or post, commenting and editing. It is left, with part 3 there, until the session changing the community code for T82 is done.
- **Counts:** `SpaceScreenTest` has 25 tests and the VS Code task "verify offline Space screens" now expects 25; `ReminderScreenTest` has 13 and `scripts/verify-android-reminders.ps1` now expects 13.

Docker stopped every container again at about 14:21 (exit 255); the existing containers were started again without rebuild, migration or reset. The dev database was at `0042`, while the code's head is `0043` (T173, in progress, not applied by this task), so `/health/ready` answers 503 until its owner applies it. |

**Boundary.** Interests are stored like other private data, not encrypted; whether a sensitive choice should be is the owner's decision. Telugu and Hindi names are machine drafts. News, ads, who posts and private Spaces stay undecided (Q22, Q23, Q24, C5).

## Remaining Gates

1. Complete broader accessibility, process-death/offline recovery, load/latency, production backup/PITR/key-custody and release-runtime qualification; the local restore drill above sets no RPO/RTO objective. Real OS clipboard integration also remains unverified by the payload-double test.
2. Resolve dependency/advisory, recovery/abuse, legal/privacy and deployment gates before real-user use. Local integration passes are not full M1/MVP or production approval.
3. Retain the unimplemented product scope: arbitrary/standing third-party grants, moderator tools and appeals, end-to-end encrypted and realtime messaging, other Space types, broader membership management, event capacity/invitations/public events, files, Agent and safety/data rights.
